# EP23 ஹிட்லர்: வீடில்லாதவனில் இருந்து ராட்சசன் வரை (Hitler: From Homeless to Monster): shot list

499 images so far (the list grows chapter by chapter). File names carry the running number, narration clip and line.

Rules: every picture is a made film still for this film; none is a photograph or a record. Hitler is shown with his real face at every age (reference pictures in out/cast, see shots/CAST.md); the people around him are played by invented actors. No flags, emblems, symbols, insignia or readable writing in any picture; every number, name, date and source is set in the edit (film/fx.mjs). Nothing in the pictures celebrates him. Full rules: shots/CAST.md.

Looks: **L** Upper Austria, 1889 to 1907; **V** Vienna, 1907 to 1913; **X** what came later (1933 to 1945); **S** one object; **W** the Western Front, 1914 to 1918; **M** Munich, 1919 to 1923; **G** Germany, 1924 to 1929; **D** Germany in the slump, 1930 to 1933; **O** setting given in the subject.


## N01

**001** `EP23_001_N01q00_vienna_snow_roofs_night.jpg` [V] (N01 q00: “வியன்னா. 1909, டிசம்பர். பனி விழற ராத்திரி.”)  
A wide view over the snow-covered roofs and domes of a great city at night, a tall cathedral spire in the distance, chimneys smoking, a few gas lamps glowing in the streets below, no people. Vienna around 1910 in winter: grand stone buildings, gas street lamps, trams and horse cabs, snow and coal smoke, men in worn overcoats and hats, no signboards, no lettering of any kind, no flags, no emblems. **004** `EP23_004_N01q01_young_man_in_queue_face.jpg` [V] refs: A (N01 q01: “வீடில்லாதவங்களுக்கான ஒரு அரசு விடுதி வாசல்ல ஒரு நீண்ட வரிசை. அதுல 20 வயசு இளைஞன் ஒருத்தன் நிக்கறான். கிழிஞ்ச கோட். பல நாளா வெட்டாத முடி. பையில ஒரு காசு இல்ல.”)  
The young man standing in a queue of homeless men in falling snow at night, seen from the front in a medium close-up: shoulders hunched, hands deep in his pockets, snow on his long hair and on the shoulders of his overcoat, his eyes lowered, he looks twenty and starved; just behind him the blurred faces of other poor men in caps and worn coats waiting in line, a gas lamp glowing out of focus. The young man is the same person as in the reference portrait: Adolf Hitler at twenty, in Vienna in 1909: boyish and clearly only twenty, with smooth unlined skin, thin and pale with hollow cheeks, a long narrow face, pale blue staring eyes, dark brown hair grown long over his collar with a lock falling across the forehead, a sparse patchy first beard on the chin and a thin moustache, in a worn dark overcoat too long for him. Vienna around 1910 in winter: grand stone buildings, gas street lamps, trams and horse cabs, snow and coal smoke, men in worn overcoats and hats, no signboards, no lettering of any kind, no flags, no emblems. Night, January 1933 in Berlin: the man in a plain dark suit and tie standing at a tall open window, seen close from the side in three-quarter profile, his face lit from below by the orange flicker of hundreds of torches and half lost in shadow, his expression cold and unreadable; far below and out of focus a vast crowd fills the street with small points of torch fire. The man is the same person as in the reference photograph: Adolf Hitler in his forties: dark brown hair with a side parting and a lock falling over the forehead, a small clipped dark moustache, pale blue hard eyes, a pale heavy face. He is never lit like a hero: the light is cold or from below and the mood is a warning. Germany between 1933 and 1945, an ominous mood, nothing celebrated: no signboards, no lettering of any kind, no flags, no emblems, no symbols on any cloth or wall, no insignia, no armbands, no salutes, crowds seen from far or in shadow, nothing celebrated.
